{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"clarions","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The clarion’s call to action has been heard.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A medieval brass instrument chiefly used as a battle signal; related to the trumpet, it had a narrow, straight pipe and a high-pitched, piercing sound."],"tags":["historical","poetic"],"topics":["entertainment","lifestyle","music"]},{"glosses":["The sound of a clarion (sense 1), or any sound resembling the loud, high-pitched note of a clarion."],"tags":["broadly","poetic"]},{"glosses":["An organ stop consisting of pipes with reeds giving a high-pitched note like that of a clarion (sense 1)."],"tags":["broadly"],"topics":["entertainment","lifestyle","music"]},{"glosses":["The middle register of the clarinet."],"tags":["broadly"],"topics":["entertainment","lifestyle","music"]},{"glosses":["A charge thought to represent a type of wind instrument, a keyboard instrument like a spinet, or perhaps a rest used by a knight to support a lance during jousting."],"synonyms":[{"word":"clarichord"},{"word":"rest"},{"word":"sufflue"}],"topics":["government","heraldry","hobbies","lifestyle","monarchy","nobility","politics"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈklæɹ.ɪ.ən/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈklæɹ.i.ən/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˈklɛɹ.i.ən/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":542977,"upstream_offset":2482972717,"upstream_record_sha256":"dbfc1906acd0189844caddd67ba5f5635fbf539d174910d418837331a0e12e84","word":"clarion"}

{"etymology_number":"2","forms":[{"form":"clarions","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"clarioning","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"clarioned","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"clarioned","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"glosses":["To announce or herald (something) using a clarion (noun sense 1)."],"tags":["rare","transitive"]},{"glosses":["To announce or herald (something) using a clarion (noun sense 1).","To announce or herald (something) clearly, especially so as to stir or unite people."],"tags":["figuratively","rare","transitive"]},{"glosses":["Of a thing: to cause (a place) to echo with a sound like that of a clarion."],"tags":["also","figuratively","rare","transitive"]},{"glosses":["To sound a clarion; also, to make a high-pitched, piercing sound like that of a clarion."],"tags":["intransitive","rare"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈklæɹ.ɪ.ən/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈklæɹ.i.ən/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˈklɛɹ.i.ən/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":542979,"upstream_offset":2482996165,"upstream_record_sha256":"03e797f492d2e823cbc6284c05034896d14b89fd6b9f65d881300fce8cf95b6d","word":"clarion"}
